After this skill: brainstorm each slice into its own approved spec before refining it. Only the epic-run skill may chain refine and claim, and only for slices a human allowlisted. After this skill: brainstorm the epic spec into a SOLID outcome statement FIRST, then fan it out with ps-release-workflow-epic-fanout. Only the epic-run skill may later chain the slices, and only those a human allowlisted. Inside AGY, leverages native subagents (invoke_subagent, Model: "flash" R1-R2, Model: "inherit" Argon R3 escalation & review, enable_subagent_tools: true). ([montionugera/ps-skills](https://github.com/montionugera/ps-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ps-release-workflow-epic-run))
- **ps-release-workflow-full-promote**: Use when the user wants the ENTIRE release turnover in one shot — "full promote", "babysit merge clean + new release", "ship the release end to end". Chains: promote (Gate 2 + PR) → babysit CI → squash-merge (prod deploy) → watch deploy → cleanup → start the NEXT release. Release-manager only. Saying "full promote" IS the standing authorization for the merge + prod deploy it contains. ALSO fires automatically, without asking, when the last feature of the work is verified and none is left (see "Standing auto-ship"). ([montionugera/ps-skills](https://github.com/montionugera/ps-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ps-release-workflow-guard))

- **pseudo-maldi-msi-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a spatial-transcriptome/MALDI-MSI integration method and no real paired metabolome data exist — split features of a 10X Visium mouse-brain section into two pseudo-sections and perturb coordinates with Gaussian translation to mimic neighboring-section morphology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-maldi-msi-simulation))
- **pseudo-marker-gene-detection**: Use when annotating scRNA-seq clusters with Seurat FindAllMarkers (Wilcoxon rank-sum) and you need to flag candidate pseudo markers by comparing against scCATCH paired-comparison gene sets before trusting annotations. - **pseudo-marker-gene-detection**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters are annotated via one-against-all marker gene tests (e.g. Seurat Wilcoxon) and you need to flag markers upregulated in multiple clusters that could cause incorrect or inconsistent cell type annotation. - **pseudo-merge**: Takes a proofread or apsa-style report file (or any markdown file using **Original:** / **Recommended:** syntax) and rewrites the original source file with git merge conflict markers so the user can accept or reject each suggested edit using VS Code or Positron's built-in merge conflict UI. Branch names are "original" and "claude-edits". Use when asked to apply edits, insert conflict markers, or set up merge resolution for a copy-edit report. Supports an optional @sec-label argument to restrict markers to one section. - **pseudo-ms2-spectrum-reconstruction**: Use when input is centroid-mode SWATH-DIA mzML with sequential variable-size MS2 windows (e.g., 10 windows, 1.0 m/z overlap, ~0.9 s cycle time) and pseudo-MS2 spectra must be reconstructed per MS1 feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-ms2-spectrum-reconstruction))
- **pseudo-msms-fragment-library-matching**: Use when annotating features from AIF LC–MS/MS data by ranking library candidates per feature using m/z error and fragment matching against pseudo-MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-msms-fragment-library-matching))
- **pseudo-msms-spectral-matching**: Use when MS1-only data (mzML/mzXML/imzML or pseudo MS/MS .mgf) must be structurally annotated or annotation propagated across molecular networks via spectral similarity, as in MASST-style cross-matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-msms-spectral-matching))
- **pseudo-msms-spectrum-cleaning**: Use when pseudo MS/MS spectra generated by ms1-id from MS1-only LC-MS data contain extra ion forms above the feature's precursor m/z and must be prepared for modified cosine molecular networking or library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-msms-spectrum-cleaning))
- **pseudo-msms-spectrum-generation**: Use when input is MS1-only data — mzML/mzXML LC-MS runs or imzML imaging acquisitions — and structural (level 2/3) annotation requires pseudo MS/MS spectra built from correlated in-source fragments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-msms-spectrum-generation))
- **pseudo-msms-spectrum-generation-from-in-source-fragments**: Use when input is MS1-only full-scan data (mzML, mzXML, or imzML) containing in-source fragments and the goal is structural (level 2/3) annotation without real MS/MS spectra — e.g. reanalysis of public repository LC-MS or MS imaging files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-msms-spectrum-generation-from-in-source-fragments))
- **pseudo-msms-spectrum-reconstruction-from-aif**: Use when processing centroid-mode LC-MS AIF (DIA) chromatograms where the analyst needs a pseudo-MS/MS spectrum for a subset of features of interest, e.g. features reaching statistical significance in an untargeted metabolomics experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-msms-spectrum-reconstruction-from-aif))
- **pseudo-necessity-exposure**: 职场沉默的自我觉察 不适用于: 用于指责身处真约束的人（贫困/疾病/胁迫） EN trigger: 我也是没办法 ([Natsummerance/skills](https://github.com/Natsummerance/skills/tree/HEAD/books/philosophy/sartre-existential-humanism-cangjie/pseudo-necessity-exposure) · ★5)
- **pseudo-paired-omics-data-evaluation**: Use when evaluating or benchmarking a spatial omics integration method (e.g. haCCA) that claims to outperform morphological alignment (STUtility, ICP) on data where no true shared spots exist, by building a pseudo-paired dataset with a known ground truth for label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-paired-omics-data-evaluation))
- **pseudo-pixel**: Turn a reference image of a character, creature, vehicle or prop into pixel-art spritesheets (idle, run, attack, drive, ...) by generating a 3D model of it (TripoSG), rigging and colouring it, turning it into a PS1-style low-poly model, animating it as for a 3D game, and rendering the motion small from 8 directions with dithered toon shading. Use when the user wants sprites or spritesheets of anything, wants to add or change animations of an existing pseudo-pixel character, or wants to change how its sheets are rendered (size, fps, palette, outline, angle). ([craigrusselltiu/pseudo-pixel](https://github.com/craigrusselltiu/pseudo-pixel/tree/HEAD/skills/pseudo-pixel) · ★7)
- **pseudo-ppl-eval**: Evaluates the ability of masked language models to predict masked amino acids in short peptide sequences, measuring how well the model captures local sequence dependencies without autoregressive assumptions. It specifically probes the model's capacity to generalize to unseen short peptides that were excluded from the reference database during training. Use when the user wants to benchmark on UniRef100-excluded peptides, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports PseudoPPL. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pseudo-ppl-eval) · ★3)
- **pseudo-random**: Use when implementing or debugging code that uses @suaveplan/pseudo-random: Deterministic seeded PRNG implementations (Mulberry32, SFC32, Xoshiro256++, CounterRng) with offset/fork/checkpoint/restore semantics. ([SuavePlan/suaveplan-skills](https://github.com/SuavePlan/suaveplan-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/suaveplan-random/skills/pseudo-random))
- **pseudo-read-fragmentation-from-reference-genome**: Use when you need a ground-truth-based accuracy comparison of two or more genome assemblies (original vs polished variants) and lack sequencing reads, by simulating long reads from a trusted reference such as CHM13 T2T. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pseudo-read-fragmentation-from-reference-genome))
- **pseudo-replicate-assignment-with-phenotypes**: Use when integrating multi-sample, multi-condition single-cell collections (e.g. millions of cells across many donors/studies) where batch effects must be removed without confounding biological differences between phenotypes or severity groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudo-replicate-assignment-with-phenotypes))
- **pseudo-replicate-bootstrapping-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or running Scanpro on unreplicated single-cell proportion data (no samples_col), or when determining how many pseudo-replicates (5-8 vs 8+) to use in bootstrapped differential composition analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudo-replicate-bootstrapping-benchmarking))
- **pseudo-siamese-cnn-inference-on-nmr-spectra**: Use when you have NMR spectral pairs of mixture components with chemical shift variation and need to identify mixture components, or to test how much ppm-level shift a trained pSCNN model tolerates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pseudo-siamese-cnn-inference-on-nmr-spectra))
- **pseudo-simulation-eval**: This evaluation probes the closed-loop planning robustness and causal reasoning of autonomous vehicle controllers by measuring their ability to handle compounding errors and distribution shifts. It combines real-world driving observations with pseudo-synthetic future scenarios generated via neural rendering to approximate interactive simulation without requiring a full physics engine. Use when the user wants to benchmark on nuPlan (navhard subset), or asks about evaluating this task. Reports EPDMS. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pseudo-simulation-eval) · ★3)
- **pseudo-skill**: Test-only Agent Skill that exercises packaging, manifest metadata and resource resolution. ([grauwolf32/contractor](https://github.com/grauwolf32/contractor/tree/HEAD/internal/agentskills/testdata/pseudo-skills/skills/pseudo-skill) · ★2)
- **pseudo-space-projection-of-scatac-cells**: Use when scATAC-seq cells (e.g., mouse cortex ISSAAC-seq) need spatial localization but no spatial ATAC-seq reference exists, and a matched scRNA-seq/ST tissue is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudo-space-projection-of-scatac-cells))
- **pseudo-space-reconstruction-from-scrna-seq**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq count matrix and a matched spatial transcriptomics reference from the same tissue type, and need to identify spatially heterogeneous cell subpopulations or pairwise cellular spatial associations from single-cell-only data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudo-space-reconstruction-from-scrna-seq))
- **pseudo-spatial-coordinate-generation-for-single-cell-multiomics**: Use when applying a spatial model (e.g., SMOPCA) to single-cell multi-omics count matrices that have no physical coordinates and you need pseudo-spatial locations derived from the transcriptomic latent space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pseudo-spatial-coordinate-generation-for-single-cell-multiomics))
- **pseudo-spatial-coordinate-simulation-on-2d-grid**: Use when benchmarking spatially aware multi-omics dimension reduction/clustering methods (e.g. SMOPCA vs SpatialPCA, scMDC) on single-cell multi-omics datasets such as CITE-seq or SMAGE-seq count matrices that lack real spatial coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pseudo-spatial-coordinate-simulation-on-2d-grid))
- **pseudo-tested-audit**: リポジトリの pseudo-tested 関数（テストに覆われているのに、本体を消しても 1 本も落ちない関数）を extreme mutation で棚卸しする ([tepshq/gauntlet](https://github.com/tepshq/gauntlet/tree/HEAD/skills/pseudo-tested-audit))
- **pseudo-time-correlation-evaluation**: Use when a cell-cycle pseudo time-series has been recovered (e.g., via reCAT's get_ordIndex/get_hmm_order/get_start pipeline) and an independent benchmark ordering such as an Oscope-recovered series exists to quantify agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudo-time-correlation-evaluation))
- **pseudo-time-method-comparison**: Use when multiple pseudo-time / cell-ordering methods must be ranked on data with known stage labels (e.g. cell cycle phases), by sweeping cluster numbers k and comparing correlation-scores against the labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pseudo-time-method-comparison))
- **pseudo-vs-anon-data**: Classifies data as pseudonymised or anonymised using Recital 26 reasonably likely test, Breyer ruling C-582/14, motivated intruder test, and WP29 Opinion 05/2014 on anonymisation techniques. Covers singling out, linkability, and inference tests. Keywords: pseudonymisation, anonymisation, Recital 26, re-identification, k-anonymity, differential privacy, WP29 Opinion 05/2014. ([mukul975/Privacy-Data-Protection-Skills](https://github.com/mukul975/Privacy-Data-Protection-Skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/data-classification-skills/skills/pseudo-vs-anon-data) · ★343 · 4 copies)
- **pseudo-vs-anon-data**: Use when classifying data as pseudonymised or anonymised using Recital 26 reasonably likely test, Breyer ruling C-582/14, motivated intruder test, and WP29 Opinion 05/2014 on anonymisation techniques. Covers singling out, linkability, and inference tests. Keywords: pseudonymisation, anonymisation, Recital 26, re-identification, k-anonymity, differential privacy, WP29 Opinion 05/2014. Do NOT use for database index optimization or generic code refactoring. ([imMamdouhaboammar/privacy-skills](https://github.com/imMamdouhaboammar/privacy-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pseudo-vs-anon-data))
- **pseudoalignment-signature-quantification**: Use when you need alignment-free quantification of transcript splicing units or detection/annotation of novel splicing events from RNA-seq reads, and prefer pseudoalignment (kallisto equivalence classes) over STAR-style align-and-count pipelines.
